Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The fungi operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 472. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 72 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the fungi space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 472 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 72. QED.
